What are the five commonly taken specimens for patients who received extirpation of matter from left lower lung lobe, via natural or artificial opening endoscopic previously during the same month since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.spec_type_desc FROM ( SELECT T2.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'extirpation of matter from left lower lung lobe, via natural or artificial opening endoscopic' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, microbiologyevents.charttime FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',microbiologyevents.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.spec_type_desc ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.spec_type_desc', 't3.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'microbiologyevents', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'extirpation of matter from left lower lung lobe, via natural or artificial opening endoscopic'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',microbiologyevents.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

Count the number of unique medicines that patient 10026354 was prescribed in 11/this year.

How many patients in 2100 received central venous catheter placement with guidance after the reopening of recent thoracotomy site procedure within the same month?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'central venous catheter placement with guidance' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'central venous catheter placement with guidance'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND strftime('%Y-%m',T2.charttime) = strftime('%Y-%m',T1.charttime) GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5 )

----------
TRUTH:
SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5 )

Count the number of patients diagnosed with gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis who did not come to the hospital within 2 months since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id NOT IN ( SELECT DISTINCT T1.subject_id FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN admissions AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T2.admittime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') ) AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) >= '2100'
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis'], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) >= '2100' AND datetime(admissions.admittime) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis'], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t2.charttime', '<', 'admissions.admittime']]}

----------

What proportion of patients with a diagnosis of acetonuria survive over a 6-month period?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acetonuria' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acetonuria'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/2 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

How many patients in 2100 received coronary arteriography using two catheters after the single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass procedure within the same hospital visit?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT p1.subject_id) 
FROM procedures_icd p1 
JOIN d_icd_procedures d1 ON p1.icd_code = d1.icd_code 
JOIN procedures_icd p2 ON p1.hadm_id = p2.hadm_id AND p1.subject_id = p2.subject_id 
JOIN d_icd_procedures d2 ON p2.icd_code = d2.icd_code 
WHERE d1.long_title = 'single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass' 
AND d2.long_title = 'coronary arteriography using two catheters' 
AND strftime('%Y', p1.charttime) = '2100' 
AND p1.charttime < p2.charttime;
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['p1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['d_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d1.long_title', '=', 'single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass'], ['d2.long_title', '=', 'coronary arteriography using two catheters'], ['p1.charttime', '<', 'p2.charttime;'], ["strftime('%y',p1.charttime)", '=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'coronary arteriography using two catheters' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'coronary arteriography using two catheters'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id']]}

Count the number of patients who had arterial catheterization in the same month after having been diagnosed with osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region and thigh in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region and thigh' ) AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'arterial catheterization' ) AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND strftime('%Y-%m', T1.charttime) = strftime('%Y-%m', T2.charttime)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'arterial catheterization'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y-%m',t1.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m', t2.charttime)"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region and thigh' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'arterial catheterization' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'arterial catheterization'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with other psoriasis?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(C1) AS survival_rate FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) as C1 FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN patients ON diagnoses_icd.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other psoriasis' ) ) AS T;
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other psoriasis']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other psoriasis' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other psoriasis'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

TRUTH:

How many patients died after having been diagnosed with facial weakness during the same hospital encounter throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions JOIN diagnoses_icd ON admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id JOIN d_icd_diagnoses ON diagnoses_icd.icd_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'facial weakness' AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year') AND admissions.dod IS NOT NULL AND admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['admissions.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ['admissions.hadm_id', '=', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'facial weakness'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year')"]]}
----------

Calculate the total number of patients diagnosed with other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use who didn't revisit the hospital within the same month since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AND NOT EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM admissions AS revisit WHERE revisit.subject_id = admissions.subject_id AND strftime('%Y-%m',revisit.admittime) = strftime('%Y-%m',admissions.admittime) AND revisit.admittime > admissions.admittime )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other'], ['revisit.admittime', '>', 'admissions.admittime'], ['revisit.subject_id', '=', 'admissions.subject_id'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y-%m',revisit.admittime)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m',admissions.admittime)"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) >= '2100' AND datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of month') )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other'], ["datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t2.charttime', '<', 'admissions.admittime']]}

What are the three most frequent drugs prescribed to female patients with an age of 20s within the same month after they were diagnosed with unspecified acquired hypothyroidism?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 AND admissions.gender = 'f' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.gender', '=', 'f'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:

Count the number of patients who were diagnosed with irritable bowel syndrome but didn't come back to the hospital within 2 months since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, admissions.dischtime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'irritable bowel syndrome' ) AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 LEFT JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, admissions.admittime FROM admissions WHERE strftime('%Y', admissions.admittime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND datetime(T2.admittime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.dischtime) AND datetime(T1.dischtime, '+2 month') WHERE T2.subject_id IS NULL
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.admittime', 'admissions.dischtime', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'irritable bowel syndrome'], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t2.subject_id', 'is', 'null']]}

Throughout this year, what are the top five most common drugs prescribed during the same hospital encounter to female patients aged 50s after being diagnosed with epilepsy, unspecified, not intractable, without status epilepticus?
----------

How many people died after being diagnosed with posttraumatic stress disorder within 2 months throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'posttraumatic stress disorder' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE ( datetime(T3.dod) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') ) OR ( datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T3.dod) AND datetime(T3.dod,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'posttraumatic stress disorder'], ["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}

What are the five most frequently prescribed medications for patients diagnosed with urethral stricture, unspecified previously within 2 months, this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'urethral stricture, unspecified' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',current_date) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y',current_date) ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(C1) AS survival_rate FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) as C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified' ) GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id ) AS T2
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

What is the number of patients who died after diagnosis of alcoholic cirrhosis of liver within the same month?
----------
PREDS:

Among those who were prescribed with pseudoephedrine, what are the three most commonly prescribed drugs at the same time in 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT DISTINCT P1.subject_id, P1.hadm_id FROM prescriptions AS P1 WHERE P1.drug = 'pseudoephedrine' AND strftime('%Y', P1.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT prescriptions.subject_id, prescriptions.hadm_id, prescriptions.drug FROM prescriptions WHERE strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id WHERE T2.drug != 'pseudoephedrine' GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['p1.hadm_id', 'p1.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.subject_id', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['p1.drug', '=', 'pseudoephedrine'], ["strftime('%y',p1.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t2.drug', '!=', 'pseudoephedrine'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------

What are the three most frequent drugs prescribed to female patients with an age of 20s within the same month after they were diagnosed with unspecified acquired hypothyroidism?
PRED: SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 AND admissions.gender = 'f' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
GOLD: SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
